User notes and answers:
- USER DECISION on B3 (ServerSession external-mode): adopt option (C) — DROP B3 entirely from this MP.

Rationale verified by reading code:
1. vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:12-14 already protects external servers — comfy_server(server_url=...) yields the URL without spawning, and the cleanup at lines 26-32 only fires when 'process' is non-None (i.e. only for managed subprocesses).
2. ServerSession in current production code ALWAYS owns its own subprocess (start() always calls _spawn_comfy_server). There is no production codepath that creates a ServerSession attached to an external URL — vibecomfy/runtime/run.py bypasses ServerSession entirely when given server_url=.
3. Therefore reload_for_nodepack_change() on ServerSession is always safe to tear down its own subprocess without external-mode branching, and adding _external + ExternalServerRestartRequired now would be unreachable mechanism (exactly what review §0 'every documented invariant needs a code-level check' said NOT to do).

Concrete plan changes for revise:
- REMOVE deliverable B3 entirely (no _external private flag, no ExternalServerRestartRequired class, no external-mode detection in ServerSession, no test for external-server-mode).
- Keep B1 (reload_for_nodepack_change), B2 (in-flight guard), B4 (vibecomfy nodes lock), B5 (doctor SHA verification), B6 (tests for B1/B2/B4/B5).
- Add ONE sentence in code comments / plan docs near the new reload_for_nodepack_change() noting: 'ServerSession external-mode (attach to a server VibeComfy didn't spawn) is deferred to MP-5 alongside session-shared multi-stage orchestration; current production paths use comfy_server(server_url=...) which already does the right thing.'
- All other deliverables (A1-A6, C1-C2) unchanged.

After revising, run critique → gate. The 6 remaining flags should collapse since they all clustered on B3.

        Approved plan:

# Implementation Plan: P1 Foundation — Typed Handles, Custom-Node Lifecycle, RunPod Smoke Scaffold

## Overview

Goal: ship MP-2 (typed `Handle`) + MP-4 (custom-node lifecycle/lockfile enforcement) + a RunPod smoke-test scaffold as one squashable PR. Land enforcement gates from the DSL plan and review without preempting MP-3, MP-5, or MP-6.

**B3 removed per user decision.** Code-grounded reasoning verified: `vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:12-32`'s `comfy_server(server_url=...)` already protects external servers (the cleanup at lines 26-32 only fires when `process` is non-None, i.e. only for managed subprocesses). `vibecomfy/runtime/run.py` bypasses `ServerSession` entirely when given `server_url=...`. There is no production codepath that creates a `ServerSession` attached to an external URL, so external-mode detection on `ServerSession` would be unreachable mechanism — the exact "every documented invariant needs a code-level check" anti-pattern review §0 warned against. External-mode handling for `ServerSession` is deferred to MP-5 alongside session-shared multi-stage orchestration.

**Doctor behavior contract clarified in v6:** `--lint` (the new MP-2/A5 flag) is default-OFF, so existing `vibecomfy doctor <path>` callers see no NEW lint output. Lockfile drift verification (B5 / Step 16) IS active by default per the brief's explicit "FAIL CLOSED" mandate — this DOES change ordinary `vibecomfy doctor <path>` output: machines with `custom_nodes.lock` present but locked packs absent now see warnings, machines with installed packs at mismatched SHAs now exit 1 unless `--allow-drift` is passed. Existing direct `_cmd_doctor(argparse.Namespace(path=...))` callers in `tests/test_cli.py` and `tests/test_doctor_models.py` must isolate from the repo's `custom_nodes.lock` (Step 17 sub-step 6 spells out the monkeypatch approach).

Repo shape that matters (verified against `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy`):
- `Handles` (plural) at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11`; values are `Mapping[str, Any]`. `Handle` (singular) does not exist yet.
- `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:8` already imports `from vibecomfy.workflow import VibeWorkflow` — placing `Handle` in `vibecomfy/blocks/` would create a circular dep with `workflow.py`. Resolution: neutral module `vibecomfy/handles.py`.
- `Handles({...})` construction sites span `subgraph.py:47`, `loaders.py`, `encoding.py`, `sampling.py`, `save.py`, `latent.py`, **`decode.py`**, **`video.py`** (full set confirmed by `Handles(` audit before coding).
- `compile("api")` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:187`) emits `[str(edge.from_node), int(edge.from_output)]` — non-numeric output names raise `ValueError`. P1 `.out()` accepts only int and digit-string slots.
- `connect`'s `to_ref` is a target input path `"node.input_name"` — only `from_ref` accepts a `Handle`.
- `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:32-47` accepts `str | VibeNode` only — must extend to `Handle`.
- `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py:185`) has no in-flight guard. `queue_prompt_api` await at `:144` is unshielded.
- `ServerSession` always owns its own subprocess in production (`start()` calls `_spawn_comfy_server()`). No external-mode detection needed in P1; MP-5 will revisit when session-shared multi-stage orchestration lands.
- `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` does not currently re-export the lifecycle classes. Resolution: export `EmbeddedSession`, `ServerSession`, `SessionConfig`, `RunResult`.
- **`vibecomfy/node_packs.py` is a flat module file (verified via `ls`), NOT a package directory.** New lockfile code lives at `vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py` (sibling module) — no package conversion required.
- **`_cmd_doctor` has direct callers in tests** (verified: `tests/test_cli.py:285`, `tests/test_doctor_models.py:54,76` all call `_cmd_doctor(argparse.Namespace(path=...))` with no other attrs). New flag access uses `getattr(args, "lint", False)` / `getattr(args, "allow_drift", False)`. Those tests also need to be isolated from the repo `custom_nodes.lock` so the new B5 default-on drift check does not bleed into their assertions (Step 17 sub-step 6).
- **`custom_nodes.lock` already pins KJNodes and WanVideoWrapper.** Drift verification is bounded: lockfile absent → skip; pack dir absent → warn-not-fail; pack dir present + SHA mismatch → fail-closed unless `--allow-drift`. **This IS a change to default `vibecomfy doctor <path>` behavior** per brief B5, and the success criteria below acknowledge it explicitly.
- pytest config at `pyproject.toml:33-34` has `asyncio_mode = "auto"` and no markers / no collection hooks.
- Parity reference: `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` plus two more anchors.
- `docs/authoring.md` currently teaches the deprecated raw-string `Handles(text=node.id)` shape — update in this PR.

Constraints (hard, unchanged):
- Backward compatibility: every existing test passes unchanged. `Handle.__eq__` accepts the bare-node-id form when `output_slot == 0` (settled SD-1).
- Old lockfile schema (`name sha url`) loads via migration shim. `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename works on read.
- No mypy-grade typing claim (P1 = typed metadata + lint).
- No serialization gate (MP-3), no schema/`output_type` integration (MP-6), no VibeFlow/`wrap_api_dict` (MP-5).
- **No external-mode handling for `ServerSession` in P1.** That is MP-5 territory.
- **Doctor adds B5 lockfile drift verification to the default `_cmd_doctor` flow.** This is mandated by the brief (`FAIL CLOSED`) and is NOT a backward-compat regression — it is the intended behavior change. Existing direct test callers monkeypatch around it (Step 17 sub-step 6).
- Smoke test is scaffolded only — never executed by the megaplan run.

## Phase A: Typed Handle Scaffold (closes review §1, §2, §3, §10)

### Step 1: Create `Handle` in a neutral module (`vibecomfy/handles.py` — new)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `vibecomfy/handles.py` (top-level, no imports from `vibecomfy.workflow` or `vibecomfy.blocks`):
   ```python
   from __future__ import annotations
   from dataclasses import dataclass

   @dataclass(frozen=True)
   class Handle:
       node_id: str
       output_slot: int | str = 0   # int or digit-string only in P1 (named outputs land in MP-6)
       output_type: str | None = None   # populated by MP-6
       name: str | None = None      # human-readable label; advisory only

       def __str__(self) -> str:
           return f"{self.node_id}.{self.output_slot}"

       def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
           if isinstance(other, Handle):
               return self.node_id == other.node_id and str(self.output_slot) == str(other.output_slot)
           if isinstance(other, str):
               canonical = f"{self.node_id}.{self.output_slot}"
               if other == canonical:
                   return True
               # Backcompat: bare node-id matches when slot == 0 (keeps tests/test_blocks.py:41 green)
               if str(self.output_slot) == "0" and other == self.node_id:
                   return True
               return False
           return NotImplemented

       def __hash__(self) -> int:
           return hash((self.node_id, str(self.output_slot)))
   ```
2. **Re-export** from `vibecomfy/__init__.py` (`from vibecomfy.handles import Handle`) so `from vibecomfy import Handle` is the canonical public path.
3. **Re-export** from `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py` for backwards-friendly access from block authors.

### Step 2: Tighten `Handles` plural wrapper with coercion warning (`vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `Handles.__init__` to coerce raw strings to `Handle`:
   - `"node.slot"` → `Handle(node_id, output_slot=slot)` (split on first `.`; preserve raw token).
   - bare `"id"` → `Handle(id, 0)`.
   - Each coercion emits `warnings.warn(..., DeprecationWarning, stacklevel=2)` exactly once per source location, keyed on `(filename, lineno)` from `inspect.stack()[1]`.
2. **Update** the `values` field annotation to `Mapping[str, Handle]`.

### Step 3: Audit and migrate ALL block construction sites (`vibecomfy/blocks/{subgraph,loaders,encoding,sampling,save,latent,decode,video}.py`, `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Audit** with `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` to enumerate every construction site before editing.
2. **Replace** every `Handles({"x": "<id>"})` and `Handles({"x": f"{node.id}.{slot}"})` with `Handles({"x": Handle(node_id=..., output_slot=..., name="x")})`. Bare-node-id stores become `Handle(node_id=node.id, output_slot=0, name="x")`.
3. **Update** `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:32-47` so `node_id()` and `connect()` accept `Handle`:
   - `node_id(value)` → if `Handle`, return `value.node_id`.
   - `connect(workflow, source, ...)` → if `source` is a `Handle`, route through `workflow.connect(source, ...)` (Handle-aware path) and ignore the legacy `output_slot` kwarg.

### Step 4: Add `wf.node(...)` typed authoring wrapper (`vibecomfy/workflow.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Import** `Handle` at top of `workflow.py` (`from vibecomfy.handles import Handle`).
2. **Add** `_NodeBuilder` and `VibeWorkflow.node`:
   ```python
   def node(self, class_type: str, **kwargs: Any) -> "_NodeBuilder":
       node = self.add_node(class_type)
       for key, value in kwargs.items():
           if isinstance(value, Handle):
               self.connect(value, f"{node.id}.{key}")
           else:
               node.inputs[key] = value
       return _NodeBuilder(workflow=self, node=node)
   ```
   `_NodeBuilder` exposes:
   - `id: str` (mirrors GraphBuilder's `id=` convention).
   - `out(slot: int | str) -> Handle` — accepts integers and digit strings only. Implementation guard: `int(str(slot))` inside try/except `ValueError`. On non-numeric input raise:
     ```python
     raise NotImplementedError(
         f"Named outputs (e.g. .out({slot!r})) require MP-6 schema integration; "
         "pass an integer slot for P1."
     )
     ```
3. **Extend** `VibeWorkflow.connect`'s `from_ref` parameter only:
   ```python
   def connect(self, from_ref: str | Handle, to_ref: str) -> "VibeWorkflow":
       ...
   ```
   Coerce `Handle` via `str(handle)` before the existing `split(".", 1)`. `to_ref` stays `str`.

### Step 5: Implement `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract (`vibecomfy/workflow.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `def run_until(self, handle: Handle) -> Any:` that raises `NotImplementedError("wf.run_until is gated on MP-6 schema integration; handle.output_type is None. Manually attach SaveImage/PreviewImage/SaveAudio.")` when `handle.output_type is None`. If `output_type` is hand-populated, raise `NotImplementedError("wf.run_until runner is not implemented yet; gated on MP-6.")`.

### Step 6: Doctor `--lint` / `--allow-drift` flags (`vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Migrate the doctor argparse** without breaking direct `_cmd_doctor(argparse.Namespace(path=...))` callers in `tests/test_cli.py:285`, `tests/test_doctor_models.py:54,76`:
   - Keep `path` as the single positional argument.
   - Add `--lint` and `--allow-drift` (`action="store_true"`, default False) on the existing parser. No subcommand group.
   - **Inside `_cmd_doctor`, read both flags via `getattr(args, "lint", False)` and `getattr(args, "allow_drift", False)`** — never `args.lint` / `args.allow_drift` directly.
2. **When `getattr(args, "lint", False)` is set**, run an AST-walker over the loaded `.py` builder source (skip JSON refs):
   - **Source-side flag:** a string literal matching `r"^\w+\.\w+$"` passed positionally to `workflow.connect(...)` when a `Handle`-returning call (`wf.node(...).out(...)`) appears earlier in the same function.
   - **Coercion-side flag:** a call expression `str(<name>)` where `<name>` was bound from `... .out(...)` in the same function.
3. **Emit** lint warnings via stdout prefixed `- untyped_raw_ref:` and exit code 0 (warning-only). **Lint behavior is default-off:** when `--lint` is unset, NO `untyped_raw_ref` output is emitted. (Lockfile drift verification per Step 16 IS default-on per brief B5 — that is a separate, brief-mandated behavior change.)
4. **Module-level helper `_read_doctor_lockfile() -> list[LockEntry]`** wraps `vibecomfy.node_packs_lockfile.read_lockfile()` so test callers can monkeypatch this single seam (see Step 17 sub-step 6). When the lockfile is absent, returns `[]` cleanly (no error).

### Step 7: Real-ready-template parity tests (`tests/parity/test_p1_typed_handle_parity.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Pick** three actual ready templates as parity anchors. Required: `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py`. Two more chosen during implementation from existing builders by structural variety.
2. **For each anchor**, write a sibling fixture under `tests/parity/fixtures/` that reproduces the same graph using only `wf.node(...).out(<int_slot>)` and Handle-aware `wf.connect(handle, "node.input")`.
3. **Assert:**

   - **GraphBuilder parity (skipped when `comfy_execution` missing):** `wf.compile("api") == wf.compile("graphbuilder")`.
   - **Corpus parity (must pass):** typed-handle build's `compile("api")` matches the **original ready template's** `compile("api")` modulo allowed differences.
4. **Do not** rewrite the original ready templates in place — that's MP-5 territory.

### Step 8: Handle/wrapper unit tests (`tests/test_handle.py` — new)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cover:**
   - `Handle.__str__`, `__eq__` against another Handle, against `"node.slot"`, against bare `"node"` (slot=0 only).
   - `__hash__` consistency for `("12", 0)` vs `("12", "0")`.
   - `Handles` coercion-with-DeprecationWarning — same call site twice → exactly one warning.
   - `from vibecomfy import Handle` import path.
   - `wf.node(...).out(0)` returns Handle that compiles cleanly through `compile("api")`.
   - `wf.node(...).out("CONDITIONING")` raises `NotImplementedError` referencing MP-6.

## Phase B: Custom-Node Lifecycle and Lockfile (closes review §6, §7)

### Step 10: In-flight task tracking with concurrent-run guard (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `self._inflight_run: asyncio.Task | None = None` to both `EmbeddedSession.__init__` and `ServerSession.__init__`.
2. **At the top of `run()` in both classes**, guard against concurrent overwrite:
   ```python
   if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done():
       raise RuntimeError(
           "session already has a run in flight; concurrent run() is not supported in P1"
       )
   self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()
   ```
   In `finally`: `self._inflight_run = None`. Wrap the entire body of `run()` so the in-flight tracker covers queue submit + watchdog finalization + metadata write.
3. **Modify** `EmbeddedSession.stop` and `ServerSession.stop` to take `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`:
   - `_inflight_run` set + done: clear the field, proceed with normal teardown.
   - `_inflight_run` set + not done + `wait_for_inflight=True`: `await self._inflight_run`. Catch and re-raise the run's own exception before tearing down.
   - `_inflight_run` set + not done + `wait_for_inflight=False`: raise `RuntimeError("session.stop() called while a run is in flight; pass wait_for_inflight=True or call after run completes")`.
   - No `_inflight_run`: existing behavior unchanged → all current `tests/test_runtime_session.py` callers stay green.

### Step 11: `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `async def reload_for_nodepack_change(self, *, reason: str) -> None`. Strict-refuse if a run is in flight:
   - If `_inflight_run` is set and not done: raise `RuntimeError("reload_for_nodepack_change refused: run in flight")`.
   - Mirror `stop()`'s `_context.__aexit__` block (preserve the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow at `:190-192`).
   - Reset `self._comfy = None`, `self._context = None`, `self._schema_provider = None`, `self._schema_warning_emitted = False`, `self.last_fingerprint = None`.
   - `await self.start()` to reopen a fresh `Comfy()` context.
   - `logger.info("reload_for_nodepack_change: %s", reason)`.

### Step 12: `ServerSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` (`vibecomfy/runtime/session.py`) — managed-mode only
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `async def reload_for_nodepack_change(self, *, reason: str) -> None` to `ServerSession`:
   - Enforce in-flight guard: if `_inflight_run` is set and not done, raise `RuntimeError("reload_for_nodepack_change refused: run in flight")`.
   - `await self.stop()` (terminates the spawned `comfyui serve` subprocess via the existing managed-mode teardown).
   - `await self.start()` (re-spawns via `_spawn_comfy_server`).
   - `logger.info("reload_for_nodepack_change: %s", reason)`.
2. **Add a one-line code comment** above the method body documenting the deferral:
   ```python
   # NOTE: ServerSession external-mode handling (attach to a server VibeComfy didn't spawn)
   # is deferred to MP-5 alongside session-shared multi-stage orchestration. Current
   # production paths route external server URLs through comfy_server(server_url=...)
   # in vibecomfy/runtime/server.py, which already skips spawn/cleanup for external URLs.
   ```
3. **No `_external` field, no `attach()` classmethod, no `ExternalServerRestartRequired` class** in P1. Brief B3 is dropped per user decision (verified: production code never instantiates `ServerSession` against an external URL, so the mechanism would be unreachable).

### Step 13: Runtime exports (`vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` to re-export `EmbeddedSession`, `ServerSession`, `SessionConfig`, `RunResult`. **Do not** export `ExternalServerRestartRequired` — it is not added in P1.

### Step 14: Lockfile schema migration + reader/writer (`vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py` — new sibling module)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create `vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py`** as a sibling module to the existing `vibecomfy/node_packs.py` flat module file. **Do not** convert `node_packs.py` into a package.
2. **Module contents:**
   - `@dataclass class LockEntry: name: str; git_commit_sha: str; url: str; semantic_label: str | None = None; source_sha256: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)`.
   - `def read_lockfile(path: Path = Path("custom_nodes.lock")) -> list[LockEntry]`: detect format. **If the file does not exist, return `[]` (no exception).** Legacy lines (3 whitespace tokens) → `LockEntry(name, git_commit_sha=token2, url=token3)`. New format (TOML, detected by leading `[` or `=`) → parse via `tomllib`. Accept `git_sha` and `git_commit_sha` synonyms when reading TOML.
   - `def write_lockfile(entries: list[LockEntry], path: Path) -> None`: always writes TOML keyed by `name`.
3. **No edits to the existing `custom_nodes.lock` in this PR**.

### Step 15: `vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command (`vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Register** new subcommand `lock` with `_cmd_nodes_lock(args)`:
   - Reads existing lockfile via `from vibecomfy.node_packs_lockfile import read_lockfile, write_lockfile, LockEntry`.
   - Resolves `semantic_label` to a `git_commit_sha` if a checkout is present (subprocess `git -C <pack_dir> rev-parse HEAD`).
   - Optional `--with-source-sha256` flag (default off) computes per-class-file `*.py` SHA-256 digests for installed packs.
   - Writes new TOML via `write_lockfile`.
2. **Update** `register(subparsers)` at `vibecomfy/commands/nodes.py:101` to add the new subparser.

### Step 16: `vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification with bounded scope (`vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** verification pass to `_cmd_doctor` (read `getattr(args, "allow_drift", False)`):
   - Read `custom_nodes.lock` via `_read_doctor_lockfile()` (the seam from Step 6 sub-step 4).
   - **Lockfile absent (empty list returned) → skip drift verification entirely.** No output, no error.
   - **Lockfile present**: for each entry, locate the installed nodepack (search `vendor/<name>` first, then any other path that `vibecomfy.node_packs` consumers use today).
     - **Pack dir absent on disk → log a warning `"<pack> in lockfile but not installed; skipping drift check"` and continue. Do NOT fail-closed.** This is the new B5 default behavior — it changes ordinary `vibecomfy doctor <path>` output relative to today's behavior.
     - **Pack dir present**: subprocess `git -C <pack_dir> rev-parse HEAD` and compare to `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` present, hash and compare.
2. **Mismatch policy (only when pack is present and SHAs differ):**
   - Default fail-closed → exit `1` with `Layer: nodepack lockfile drift`.
   - `--allow-drift` → emit warnings, exit `0`.
3. **Wiring:** runs *before* the existing schema-validation pass so a real drift error short-circuits cleanly. **This is a brief-mandated default-on behavior change for `vibecomfy doctor <path>`** — see "Doctor behavior contract clarified in v6" in the Overview.

### Step 17: Lifecycle, lockfile, and doctor tests (`tests/test_runtime_session.py` extensions, `tests/test_nodes_lock.py` — new, `tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py` — new, plus monkeypatch fixture for existing doctor tests)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **In-flight guard** (extend `tests/test_runtime_session.py`): use the existing `FakeComfy` fixture; arrange `queue_prompt_api` to block on an `asyncio.Event`; assert (a) `stop(wait_for_inflight=False)` raises `RuntimeError`; (b) `stop(wait_for_inflight=True)` waits cleanly; (c) exceptions raised by the in-flight `run()` task propagate through `stop(wait_for_inflight=True)` before context teardown; (d) **concurrent `run()` while another is in-flight raises `RuntimeError("session already has a run in flight; ...")`**.
2. **Reload verb (embedded):** assert `reload_for_nodepack_change(reason="test")` calls `__aexit__` then `__aenter__`; `_comfy` is a fresh instance; in-flight refusal triggers when a run is pending.
3. **Reload verb (server, managed mode):** with mocked `_spawn_comfy_server`, assert `await s.reload_for_nodepack_change(reason="test")` calls `stop()` then `start()` (mock both, verify call order). In-flight refusal triggers when a run is pending. **No external-mode test** — that path doesn't exist in P1.
4. **Lockfile round-trip** (`tests/test_nodes_lock.py`): write a legacy 3-token line, read it, write back as TOML, read TOML, assert parity. Confirm `git_sha` → `git_commit_sha` rename works on TOML read. Confirm `read_lockfile(absent_path)` returns `[]` cleanly.
5. **SHA verification** (`tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py`): create a fake pack dir; monkeypatch the `git rev-parse HEAD` subprocess. Five cases:
   - (a) lockfile absent → exit 0;
   - (b) lockfile present, pack dir absent → exit 0 with warning;
   - (c) match → exit 0;
   - (d) mismatch without `--allow-drift` → exit 1;
   - (e) mismatch with `--allow-drift` → exit 0 with warning.
6. **Isolate existing direct `_cmd_doctor` callers from the repo `custom_nodes.lock`** (extend `tests/test_cli.py:285`, `tests/test_doctor_models.py:54,76`):
   - In each of those three test functions (or via a shared `conftest.py` fixture in `tests/`), add `monkeypatch.setattr("vibecomfy.commands.doctor._read_doctor_lockfile", lambda: [])` before invoking `_cmd_doctor(argparse.Namespace(path=...))`.
   - This isolates those tests from the new B5 default-on behavior so they keep asserting their existing scenarios (model checks, schema validation) without being polluted by absent-pack warnings or SHA-drift errors derived from the repo's pinned `ComfyUI-KJNodes`/`ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`.
   - Document this with a comment block in the fixture: `# B5 lockfile drift verification is default-on per Step 16; this test predates B5 and isolates from the seam to keep its existing assertions stable.`

## Phase C: RunPod Smoke-Test Scaffold (true deselection)

### Step 18: Pytest opt-in gate with TRUE deselection (`tests/conftest.py` — new, `pyproject.toml`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add `tests/conftest.py`** with a real opt-in gate that deselects (not skips) runpod-marked items:
   ```python
   import pytest

   def pytest_addoption(parser):
       parser.addoption(
           "--runpod",
           action="store_true",
           default=False,
           help="Run opt-in RunPod GPU smoke tests (provisions real pods; requires RUNPOD_API_KEY).",
       )

   def pytest_collection_modifyitems(config, items):
       if config.getoption("--runpod"):
           return
       selected = []
       deselected = []
       for item in items:
           if "runpod" in item.keywords:
               deselected.append(item)
           else:
               selected.append(item)
       if deselected:
           config.hook.pytest_deselected(items=deselected)
           items[:] = selected
   ```
2. **Register the marker** in `pyproject.toml`:
   ```toml
   markers = [
     "runpod: opt-in smoke tests that provision real RunPod GPU pods (requires --runpod and RUNPOD_API_KEY).",
   ]
   ```

### Step 19: Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `tests/smoke/__init__.py` (empty) and `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py`.
2. **Test body:**
   - Module-top: `pytestmark = pytest.mark.runpod`.
   - Test-top: skip if `os.environ.get("RUNPOD_API_KEY")` is unset.
   - Import `runpod_lifecycle` (handle `ImportError` → `pytest.skip` referencing `VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT`).
   - Provision one cheap pod (RTX 4090 or A40). Wrap in `try/finally` so teardown runs on success **and** failure.
   - On the pod: install vibecomfy from current branch, build a 3-node typed-Handle workflow, call `await session.reload_for_nodepack_change(reason="smoke")`, then `await session.run(wf)`.
   - Assert at least one output path exists in `result.outputs`.
   - Tear down pod in `finally`.
3. **Do not call this test in this megaplan run.**

### Step 20: Document the smoke contract (`docs/runpod_smoke.md` — new, plus pointer in `docs/custom_nodes.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Document**: how to run (`pytest --runpod -m runpod tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py`), required env (`RUNPOD_API_KEY`, optional `VIBECOMFY_RUNPOD_LIFECYCLE_ROOT`), expected wall-clock + cost (~5–10 min, ~$0.05–$0.20), and the safety contract.

## Execution Order

1. Phase A (Handle module → tightened `Handles` → `wf.node().out()` → full block migration sweep → parity fixtures → authoring-docs update → lint flag with `getattr` defaults + `_read_doctor_lockfile` seam).
2. Phase B (in-flight task tracking with concurrent-run guard → embedded reload verb → server reload verb (managed-mode only, with MP-5 deferral comment) → runtime exports → sibling lockfile module → CLI lock subcommand → doctor SHA verification with bounded scope → lifecycle/lockfile tests → existing-doctor-test isolation via `_read_doctor_lockfile` monkeypatch).
3. Phase C (conftest with true `pytest_deselected` → marker registration → smoke file → docs).

## Validation Order

1. `pytest tests/test_handle.py` — fastest signal.
2. `pytest tests/test_blocks.py tests/test_workflow_core.py` — Handle migration didn't regress block authoring or compile.
3. `pytest tests/parity/` — typed-handle parity against real ready templates.
4. `pytest tests/test_runtime_session.py tests/test_nodes_lock.py tests/test_doctor_lockfile.py` — Phase B.
5. `pytest tests/test_cli.py tests/test_doctor_models.py` — confirms the existing direct `_cmd_doctor(argparse.Namespace(path=...))` callers still pass after the Step 17 sub-step 6 monkeypatch isolation. (These tests now exercise lint/drift defaults via `getattr` + the `_read_doctor_lockfile` seam returning `[]`.)
6. `pytest` (full suite, no flags) — every existing test passes unchanged AND runpod tests are deselected.
7. `pytest --collect-only` (no flags) — runpod test does NOT appear in the collected items list.
8. `pytest --runpod --collect-only -m runpod` — runpod test appears in the collected items list.
9. Manual sanity checks: `vibecomfy doctor --lint <fixture>` flags `untyped_raw_ref`; `vibecomfy doctor PATH` on a machine WITHOUT KJNodes/WanVideoWrapper installed now warns (per B5 default-on) but does not fail; `vibecomfy nodes lock` rewrites the lockfile in TOML; `vibecomfy doctor PATH` with a synthetic SHA-mismatched pack dir exits 1, and with `--allow-drift` exits 0.

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance and judgment calls:\n\nORDER MATTERS: T1 (Handle module) is the bedrock \u2014 every other phase-A task depends on it. Build T1 first and verify the import path works (`python -c \"from vibecomfy import Handle\"`) before touching anything else. Phase B is largely independent of Phase A and can be developed in parallel; T17 is the integration point.\n\nDOCTOR SEAM: The most subtle correctness item is the _read_doctor_lockfile() seam (T6) and the matching monkeypatch in T17 sub-step 6. The plan went through six iterations to converge on this design. Implement the seam as a top-level module function (not nested inside _cmd_doctor) so monkeypatch.setattr can target it. Recommend a shared autouse fixture in a tests/ conftest scoped to test_cli.py and test_doctor_models.py rather than per-test monkeypatch \u2014 cleaner and prevents drift if new doctor tests are added.\n\nBLOCK MIGRATION SWEEP: Run `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` BEFORE editing T3, save the list, and check it off as you go. Easy to miss decode.py or video.py \u2014 both surfaced as iter-2 flag. The brief was explicit that the audit must be exhaustive.\n\nPARITY FIXTURES: Pre-pick anchors #2 and #3 before writing T7. flux2_klein_4b_t2i is required; suggest a Z-Image or SDXL variant + one audio/video template for structural variety. Document the choice in the test docstring so reviewers see the rationale.\n\nCONNECT SIGNATURE: For T4, vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:connect (the helper) is called by block helpers, not user code. Preserve the legacy str + explicit output_slot kwarg branch; only the Handle-source branch is new. Don't break existing block helper callers.\n\nCONCURRENT RUN GUARD: T10's check-then-set sequence is racy if there's no lock, but Python asyncio's single-threaded event loop makes the check-and-set atomic between awaits. The guard is `if self._inflight_run is not None and not self._inflight_run.done(): raise RuntimeError(...)` followed immediately by `self._inflight_run = asyncio.current_task()` \u2014 no awaits between, so this is correct under asyncio semantics.\n\nRUNPOD CONFTEST: T18's pytest_collection_modifyitems must call BOTH `config.hook.pytest_deselected(items=deselected)` AND `items[:] = selected`. The hook reports deselection in pytest output ('=== N deselected ==='); the slice assignment is what actually removes them from the run. Skipping just the hook means tests still execute; skipping just the slice means deselection happens but isn't reported.\n\nDO NOT EXECUTE THE RUNPOD SMOKE TEST. The test scaffold's existence is the deliverable; running it provisions real GPU pods. T19's verdict is satisfied by file presence + structure, not by a green run.\n\nPR SHAPE: This will be a large diff \u2014 handles.py (new) + 8 blocks files + workflow.py + session.py + 2 commands + node_packs_lockfile.py (new) + ~10 test files + 2 docs. Tag the PR body with deliverable IDs A1\u2013A6, B1, B2, B4, B5, B6, C1\u2013C2 so reviewers can map sections to the brief.\n\nDEFERRED ITEMS (do not implement): MP-3 compile-time serialization gate, expanded block library; MP-5 VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, wrap_api_dict, flow_id/stage_index in RunResult, ServerSession external-mode handling; MP-6 object_info snapshot, SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY, named-output-to-slot resolution.",

  "assumptions": [
    "`Handle` lives at `vibecomfy/handles.py` (top-level neutral module). Both `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py` and `vibecomfy/workflow.py` import from there. Re-exported from `vibecomfy.__init__` and `vibecomfy.blocks` for ergonomics.",
    "P1 `.out()` accepts int and digit-string slots only. Non-numeric names raise `NotImplementedError` referencing MP-6.",
    "**B3 is dropped per user decision.** No `_external` field, no `attach()` API, no `ExternalServerRestartRequired` class. ServerSession.reload_for_nodepack_change() is managed-mode only.",
    "RunPod opt-in gate uses `pytest_deselected` + `items[:] = selected` for true deselection.",
    "`Handle.__eq__` accepts the bare-node-id string form when `output_slot == 0` to keep `tests/test_blocks.py:41` passing \u2014 settled SD-1.",
    "Lockfile new format is TOML; legacy whitespace-triple format remains readable for migration via auto-detect. `read_lockfile(absent_path)` returns `[]` cleanly with no exception. Existing `custom_nodes.lock` is not touched in this PR.",
    "Lockfile module is `vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py` (sibling module to the existing flat `vibecomfy/node_packs.py`). No package conversion.",
    "Doctor argparse stays single-positional with new `--lint` and `--allow-drift` boolean flags. Inside `_cmd_doctor`, both flags are read via `getattr(args, '<name>', False)`.",
    "**`--lint` is default-OFF**: existing `vibecomfy doctor <path>` callers see no new `untyped_raw_ref` output unless they explicitly pass `--lint`.",
    "**Lockfile drift verification is default-ON per brief B5 mandate**: existing `vibecomfy doctor <path>` callers WILL see new behavior \u2014 absent-pack warnings (no fail) and pack-present + SHA-mismatch failures (exit 1 unless `--allow-drift`). This is intentional brief-mandated behavior, not a regression.",
    "**`_read_doctor_lockfile()` seam**: `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` exposes a module-level helper that wraps `vibecomfy.node_packs_lockfile.read_lockfile()`. This is the single monkeypatch point for tests. Existing direct test callers in `tests/test_cli.py:285` and `tests/test_doctor_models.py:54,76` use `monkeypatch.setattr('vibecomfy.commands.doctor._read_doctor_lockfile', lambda: [])` to isolate from the repo's `custom_nodes.lock` entries.",
    "Concurrent-run guard: `run()` raises `RuntimeError` if `_inflight_run` is set and not done.",
    "Parity-test corpus anchors: `flux2_klein_4b_t2i` is required; the other two are picked during implementation based on graph simplicity.",
    "Block-migration sweep uses `grep -RIn 'Handles(' vibecomfy/blocks/` as the authoritative enumeration before any edits.",
    "`run()` in-flight tracking uses `asyncio.current_task()` set at the top of `run()` and cleared in `finally`.",
    "Flux 4B builder is NOT rewritten in place; the typed-handle mirror lives under `tests/parity/fixtures/`.",
    "ServerSession.reload_for_nodepack_change() in managed mode is tested by mocking `_spawn_comfy_server` and verifying stop/start call order. No external-mode test."
  ],

      "id": "issue_hints-1",
      "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: Cross-checked deliverable A6 against the plan's Phase A Step 6. The brief asks for parity tests for at least three ready templates, with `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as an anchor, but the plan describes adding three small synthetic builder fixtures that only mirror ready-template semantics. That is a scope divergence: synthetic fixtures can prove `wf.node(...).out(...)` compiles, but they do not prove parity against the actual ready-template corpus requested by acceptance.",
      "category": "completeness",
      "severity_hint": "likely-significant",
      "evidence": "Moved `Handle` to a neutral `vibecomfy/handles.py` module to break the circular-import risk (FLAG-004). Restricted `.out()` to int / digit-string slots in P1 with a clear `NotImplementedError` for named outputs that references MP-6 \u2014 closes the `int(edge.from_output)` compile bug (FLAG-002 / correctness-1) and updates the example accordingly. Replaced credential-based skip with a real opt-in gate in `tests/conftest.py` (`--runpod` option + `pytest_collection_modifyitems` deselection), so default `pytest` cannot execute the RunPod test even with `RUNPOD_API_KEY` set (FLAG-003 / issue_hints-2 / all_locations-1). Added explicit `_external: bool` field on `ServerSession` plus an `attach()` constructor for external mode so reload no longer misfires on owned-but-stopped sessions (FLAG-005 / issue_hints-3). Restricted `connect`'s `Handle` acceptance to `from_ref` only \u2014 `to_ref` stays `str` (correctness-3). Expanded the explicit block-migration list to include `decode.py` and `video.py`, added a `grep` audit step, and updated `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:node_id`/`connect` typing to accept `Handle` (scope-1, callers-1). Replaced synthetic parity fixtures with three real-ready-template anchors (flux2_klein_4b_t2i + two more) and added a corpus-parity assertion that the typed-handle build matches the original ready-template's `compile(\"api\")` modulo the allowed-difference set (FLAG-001 / issue_hints-1). Added a docs-update step for `docs/authoring.md` (scope-2). Spelled out the doctor argparse migration: `--lint` and `--allow-drift` flags directly on the existing single-positional parser, no subcommand group (all_locations-3). Added explicit `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` re-exports for `EmbeddedSession`/`ServerSession`/`SessionConfig`/`ExternalServerRestartRequired` (all_locations-2). Tracked the entire `run()` task (not just the inner queue future) for in-flight, so `stop(wait_for_inflight=True)` doesn't tear down before watchdog/metadata finalization (callers-2).   "signals": {
    "iteration": 6,
    "idea": "P1 Foundation: typed Handle scaffold + custom-node lifecycle/lockfile + RunPod smoke-test scaffold.\n\nThis is the combined first phase of implementing the Python composition DSL designed in `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` (recently revised \u2014 read the current version) and addressing the issues called out in `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md`. It folds together two originally-separate efforts (MP-2 typed Handles, MP-4 lifecycle/lockfile) plus a RunPod-based smoke-test scaffold so the whole thing can ship as one squashable PR.\n\nTwo later phases will build on this output: (a) MP-3 will expand the block library and add the compile-time serialization gate; (b) MP-5 will introduce VibeFlow multi-stage orchestration. Do NOT preempt their scope.\n\nDELIVERABLES \u2014 all must land:\n\n### A. Typed Handle scaffold (closes review \u00a71, \u00a72, \u00a73, \u00a710)\n\nA1. `Handle` dataclass \u2014 frozen, fields: `node_id` (str), `output_slot` (str | int), `output_type` (str | None \u2014 populated later by MP-6 schema integration), `name` (str | None).\n   - `__str__` returns `f\"{node_id}.{output_slot}\"` matching the existing string-ref convention (see `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py:47` precedent and `vibecomfy/workflow.py:128-132` consumer that splits on `.`).\n   - `__eq__` matches BOTH another Handle (by node_id+slot) AND its string form, so `handle == \"12.0\"` works without manual `str()`. Closes the silent type-erasure hole.\n   - `__hash__` consistent with `__eq__`.\n\nA2. Tighten `Handles` (plural wrapper at `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py:11-35`) \u2014 change value type from `Mapping[str, Any]` to `Mapping[str, Handle]`. Update construction sites in `vibecomfy/blocks/` (notably `subgraph.py`) to wrap string refs as Handle instances. Backward-compat: callers passing raw strings must be coerced with a `DeprecationWarning` once per call site.\n\nA3. `wf.node(class_type, **kwargs)` \u2014 typed authoring wrapper over `VibeWorkflow.add_node(...)` (`vibecomfy/workflow.py:122-126`). Returns an object with `.out(name_or_index) -> Handle` and `.id` (matches GraphBuilder's `id=` convention). Per the revised plan's API Concepts, this is the canonical authoring entry point. Does NOT promise mypy-grade safety; `T` parameterization deferred to a later phase that commits to overloads or codegen.\n\nA4. `wf.run_until(handle)` raise contract \u2014 implement the method signature, raise `NotImplementedError` with a clear message if `handle.output_type is None`. Documented now to prevent downstream code from forming against partial behavior before MP-6 ungates it.\n\nA5. Lint/doctor coverage of `str(handle)` coercion \u2014 extend `vibecomfy doctor` (search `vibecomfy/cli/`) to flag:\n   - Raw string refs in block code where a typed Handle is in scope (warning).\n   - Sites where `str(handle)` flows into `connect()` or dict keys (warning).\n   New rule name per plan \u00a7\"Lint / Guardrail Ideas\": `untyped_raw_ref`.\n\nA6. Parity tests for at least 3 ready templates: build via `wf.node(...).out(...)`, assert `compile(\"api\")` matches `compile(\"graphbuilder\")` (modulo allowed-difference set: node IDs may move, MarkdownNote may be stripped). Use `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` as one anchor.\n\n### B. Custom-node lifecycle and lockfile enforcement (closes review \u00a76, \u00a77)\n\nB1. `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` \u2014 new explicit verb. Refuses while a `run()` is in flight (see B2), closes existing `_comfy` context (mirror `stop()` at session.py:185-195 including the `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError swallow), reopens fresh `_comfy` (mirror `start()` at session.py:103-109), resets cached state carrying stale `NODE_CLASS_MAPPINGS` references. Implement equivalent for `ServerSession` (tear down and respawn the comfyui serve subprocess).\n\nB2. **In-flight guard** on `session.stop()` and `reload_for_nodepack_change()` \u2014 today `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (session.py:185-195) doesn't check if a `run()` is awaiting `queue_prompt_api`. Add a tracking field (e.g. `_inflight_run: asyncio.Future | None`) and have stop() either refuse with a clear `RuntimeError`, OR cancel-and-await. Audit current `session.stop()` call sites; if both options reasonable, expose `wait_for_inflight: bool = True`.\n\nB3. **`ServerSession` external-mode handling** \u2014 when the session targets an external-managed `comfyui serve` (one VibeComfy didn't spawn), `reload_for_nodepack_change()` MUST NOT terminate it. Raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` (new error class) with a clear message. Today no detection path or error class exists \u2014 both must be created.\n\nB4. **`vibecomfy nodes lock` CLI command** \u2014 write `custom_nodes.lock` after resolving any `semantic_label` values. Schema per plan \u00a7\"Custom Nodes \u2014 Pinning, Hashes, and Lockfiles\":\n   - `git_commit_sha` (required primary pin \u2014 Git commit SHA-1, immutable).\n   - `semantic_label` (optional advisory, e.g. \"v1.4.0\", \"main\").\n   - `source_sha256` (optional per-node-class content digest, hex).\n   Today's lockfile records `(name, sha, url)` only \u2014 see entries for `ComfyUI-KJNodes`, `ComfyUI-WanVideoWrapper`. Extend format and migrate existing entries. Preserve `git_sha` \u2192 `git_commit_sha` rename with backward-compat read.\n\nB5. **`vibecomfy doctor` SHA verification verb** \u2014 read lockfile before `session.start()` (or as separate doctor invocation); verify each installed nodepack's git HEAD matches `git_commit_sha`. If `source_sha256` present, verify the named class file's content hash. Default on mismatch: **FAIL CLOSED**. Provide `--allow-drift` opt-in to downgrade to warning.\n\nB6. **Tests**: in-flight guard, reload verb (with fake nodepack), external-server detection (assert `ExternalServerRestartRequired` raised, external process untouched), lockfile round-trip, SHA verification (synthetic mismatch fails closed without `--allow-drift`, warns with it).\n\n### C. RunPod smoke-test scaffold (new)\n\nC1. Add `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or `tests/runpod/`, follow existing test layout). The test:\n   - Uses the `runpod_lifecycle` Python package (already available in this workspace; check `pip show runpod_lifecycle` or look in the broader workspace for the package). The package provisions ephemeral RunPod GPU pods, runs work, and tears them down.\n   - Provisions one pod (cheap GPU class \u2014 e.g. RTX 4090 or A40), installs vibecomfy on it, runs ONE representative ready template through the new `wf.node(...).out(...)` typed-Handle authoring path, submits via `EmbeddedSession.run(...)` after a `reload_for_nodepack_change()` call (to exercise B1 + B2 paths), asserts the run produces expected outputs, tears the pod down.\n   - Marked `@pytest.mark.runpod` so it's OPT-IN \u2014 never runs in default `pytest` invocations or CI without explicit opt-in.\n   - Documented in the relevant test README or `docs/`: how to run it (`pytest -m runpod`), what credentials are required (RunPod API key env var), expected wall-clock cost, and the safety contract that it tears down on success AND failure.\n\nC2. **DO NOT EXECUTE the smoke test as part of this megaplan run.** Provisioning real GPU pods burns credentials and budget \u2014 the megaplan executor must not do this. The deliverable is the test code + docs; the user (or post-merge CI) runs it explicitly.\n\n### CRITICAL CONSTRAINTS\n\n- **Backward compatibility is non-negotiable.** All existing tests must pass unchanged. Existing blocks returning `Handles({\"image\": \"12.0\"})` must keep working; tightening must coerce-with-warning. Existing `custom_nodes.lock` must still load (handle `git_sha` \u2192 `git_commit_sha` migration). Existing `session.stop()` callers must keep working.\n- Do NOT implement the compile-time serialization gate (MP-3 territory).\n- Do NOT implement schema validation, `/object_info` snapshot, or `SCHEMA_TYPE_REGISTRY` (MP-6 territory).\n- Do NOT implement VibeFlow, ExternalPythonNode, `wrap_api_dict`, multi-stage orchestration, or stage-aware run-dir naming / `flow_id` / `stage_index` in `RunResult` (MP-5 territory; SD-013 in the plan).\n- Do NOT expand the block library beyond what's needed for parity tests (MP-3 territory).\n- Hot-loading a nodepack into a running session WITHOUT reload is explicitly out of scope per the plan.\n- The RunPod smoke test must NOT be invoked during the megaplan execute phase.\n\n### KEY FILES (read first, before planning)\n\n- `docs/python_composition_dsl_plan.md` \u2014 full design (recently revised: includes new \"Desired Authoring Experience\" north-star section, API Concepts collapse, SD-013 lifecycle settlement, ExternalPythonNode marked Aspirational/P3, \"Enforcement Gates\" section, \"End-to-End Implementation Guidance\" section).\n- `docs/python_composition_dsl_review.md` \u2014 issues this MP closes.\n- `vibecomfy/blocks/__init__.py` \u2014 current `Handles` + `Block` protocol.\n- `vibecomfy/blocks/subgraph.py` \u2014 string-form handle precedent at line 47.\n- `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py` \u2014 existing provenance pattern (preserve it).\n- `vibecomfy/workflow.py` \u2014 `VibeWorkflow` IR, `add_node` (122-126), `connect` (128-132), `compile` (176-188), graphbuilder backend.\n- `vibecomfy/runtime/session.py` \u2014 especially:\n  - 88-195: `EmbeddedSession`\n  - 198-299: `ServerSession`\n  - 144: `queue_prompt_api` (currently unshielded)\n  - 185-195: `EmbeddedSession.stop()` (no in-flight check)\n  - 281-290: `ServerSession.reconfigure` (only argv changes)\n  - 487-507: `_run_metadata`\n- `vibecomfy/cli/` (search for `vibecomfy nodes` and `vibecomfy doctor` command modules).\n- `custom_nodes.lock` \u2014 current entries.\n- `docs/custom_nodes.md` \u2014 existing custom-node docs.\n- `ready_templates/image/flux2_klein_4b_t2i.py` \u2014 parity test anchor.\n- For RunPod scaffolding: locate the `runpod_lifecycle` package (try `pip show runpod_lifecycle`, search `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/` for the package source, or check the workspace's `pyproject.toml` for dependency reference). Read its README for the provisioning API.\n\n### ACCEPTANCE\n\n- All existing tests pass unchanged.\n- New parity tests pass for 3+ ready templates.\n- `Handle` is importable from a clean public path.\n- `wf.node(...).out(...)` returns a Handle that compiles cleanly through `compile(\"api\")`.\n- `wf.run_until(handle)` raises `NotImplementedError` until `output_type` is populated.\n- Doctor flags at least one synthetic `str(handle)` coercion case as `untyped_raw_ref` warning.\n- New tests for in-flight guard, reload verb, external-server detection, lockfile round-trip, SHA verification all pass.\n- `vibecomfy nodes lock` produces a lockfile in the new schema; existing entries migrate cleanly.\n- `vibecomfy doctor` fails closed on synthetic SHA drift; succeeds with `--allow-drift`.\n- `EmbeddedSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` is exposed and documented.\n- `tests/smoke/test_p1_runpod.py` (or equivalent) exists, is marked `@pytest.mark.runpod`, contains the smoke flow described in C1, and is documented for opt-in execution. The test is NOT executed as part of acceptance \u2014 only its existence and shape are verified.",

        "id": "FLAG-001",
        "concern": "Ready-template parity: plan substitutes synthetic `wf.node(...).out(...)` fixtures for the requested parity tests against at least three actual ready templates, so acceptance A6 would not be proven against the corpus.",
        "resolution": "Moved `Handle` to a neutral `vibecomfy/handles.py` module to break the circular-import risk (FLAG-004). Restricted `.out()` to int / digit-string slots in P1 with a clear `NotImplementedError` for named outputs that references MP-6 \u2014 closes the `int(edge.from_output)` compile bug (FLAG-002 / correctness-1) and updates the example accordingly. Replaced credential-based skip with a real opt-in gate in `tests/conftest.py` (`--runpod` option + `pytest_collection_modifyitems` deselection), so default `pytest` cannot execute the RunPod test even with `RUNPOD_API_KEY` set (FLAG-003 / issue_hints-2 / all_locations-1). Added explicit `_external: bool` field on `ServerSession` plus an `attach()` constructor for external mode so reload no longer misfires on owned-but-stopped sessions (FLAG-005 / issue_hints-3). Restricted `connect`'s `Handle` acceptance to `from_ref` only \u2014 `to_ref` stays `str` (correctness-3). Expanded the explicit block-migration list to include `decode.py` and `video.py`, added a `grep` audit step, and updated `vibecomfy/blocks/_utils.py:node_id`/`connect` typing to accept `Handle` (scope-1, callers-1). Replaced synthetic parity fixtures with three real-ready-template anchors (flux2_klein_4b_t2i + two more) and added a corpus-parity assertion that the typed-handle build matches the original ready-template's `compile(\"api\")` modulo the allowed-difference set (FLAG-001 / issue_hints-1). Added a docs-update step for `docs/authoring.md` (scope-2). Spelled out the doctor argparse migration: `--lint` and `--allow-drift` flags directly on the existing single-positional parser, no subcommand group (all_locations-3). Added explicit `vibecomfy/runtime/__init__.py` re-exports for `EmbeddedSession`/`ServerSession`/`SessionConfig`/`ExternalServerRestartRequired` (all_locations-2). Tracked the entire `run()` task (not just the inner queue future) for in-flight, so `stop(wait_for_inflight=True)` doesn't tear down before watchdog/metadata finalization (callers-2).         "resolution": "Five mechanical fixes layered on the v2 plan. (1) Lockfile module path changed from `vibecomfy/node_packs/lockfile.py` to `vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py` \u2014 sibling module, no package conversion needed (FLAG-006 / correctness-1 / all_locations-1). (2) Doctor flag access spelled out as `getattr(args, \"lint\", False)` / `getattr(args, \"allow_drift\", False)` so the existing direct `_cmd_doctor(argparse.Namespace(path=...))` callers in `tests/test_cli.py:285` and `tests/test_doctor_models.py:54,76` keep passing (FLAG-007 / correctness-2 / callers-2). (3) Concurrent-run guard added: `run()` raises `RuntimeError` if `_inflight_run` is set and not done, before reassigning the field \u2014 prevents the second concurrent run from masking the first (FLAG-010 / callers-1). (4) RunPod conftest hook switched from `item.add_marker(skip_runpod)` to true `pytest_deselected` + `items[:] = selected` deselection so default `pytest --collect-only` does not show the test as collected \u2014 satisfies the literal \"does NOT collect\" success criterion (FLAG-009 / all_locations-2 / issue_hints). (5) Doctor lockfile drift scope bounded: lockfile absent \u2192 skip; pack dir absent \u2192 warning only (do not fail-closed); pack dir present + SHA mismatch \u2192 fail-closed unless `--allow-drift` \u2014 avoids breaking `vibecomfy doctor PATH` on dev machines without KJNodes/WanVideoWrapper installed (FLAG-008 / scope).         "concern": "Session in-flight tracking: tracking `asyncio.current_task()` without a run-start guard lets concurrent `run()` calls overwrite `_inflight_run`, so stop/reload can miss an earlier active run.",

(1) Lockfile module path changed from `vibecomfy/node_packs/lockfile.py` to `vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py` \u2014 sibling module, no package conversion needed (FLAG-006 / correctness-1 / all_locations-1). Plan structure preserved (3 phases, 20 numbered steps, Execution + Validation orders)."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-011",
        "concern": "External server detection path: v4 removes the public `attach()` API and limits `_external` to a private flag set by tests/future callers via direct attribute assignment, so there is still no production detection path that marks a `ServerSession` as externally managed and makes `reload_for_nodepack_change()` raise `ExternalServerRestartRequired` in normal use.",
        "resolution": "Per user directive (verified by reading `vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:12-32` and `vibecomfy/runtime/run.py`): dropped deliverable B3 entirely. No `_external` private flag, no `ExternalServerRestartRequired` exception class, no external-mode detection in `ServerSession`, no external-server-mode tests. The user verified that `comfy_server(server_url=...)` already protects external servers (cleanup only fires for spawned subprocesses), and `ServerSession` is never instantiated against an external URL in production code, so the mechanism would be unreachable \u2014 the exact \"every documented invariant needs a code-level check\" anti-pattern review \u00a70 warned against. Step 12 simplified to: `ServerSession.reload_for_nodepack_change()` is managed-mode only (in-flight guard + `stop()` + `start()`), with a one-line code comment documenting the MP-5 deferral and pointing to `comfy_server(server_url=...)` as the existing external-server protection. Step 13 dropped `ExternalServerRestartRequired` from runtime exports.         Settled decisions (verify the executor implemented these correctly):
- SD-1: Backward-compat for `Handle.__eq__` accepts the bare-node-id string form when `output_slot == 0`; strict `__hash__`/`__eq__` invariant intentionally relaxed (DEBT-001/DEBT-002). (Required for `tests/test_blocks.py:41`. Settled in iter-1 gate.)
- SD-2: P1 does NOT parameterize `Handle` with `T`. (Brief A3 explicitly defers.)
- SD-3: RunPod smoke test is scaffolded but not executed during megaplan run. (Brief constraint C2.)
- SD-4: Lockfile new format is TOML; legacy whitespace-triple readable for migration. (Settled.)
- SD-5: `Handle` lives in neutral module `vibecomfy/handles.py`. (Closed FLAG-004/correctness-2/DEBT-006/DEBT-009. Settled.)
- SD-6: P1 `.out()` accepts only int and digit-string slots; non-numeric raises `NotImplementedError`. (Closed FLAG-002. Settled.)
- SD-7: `connect`'s `Handle` acceptance restricted to `from_ref` only. (Closed correctness-3. Settled.)
- SD-8: B3 (ServerSession external-mode handling) is DROPPED from P1. External-mode deferred to MP-5. Per-user verified via reading `vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:12-32` and `vibecomfy/runtime/run.py`. (User tiebreaker resolution at iter-4. Settled.)
- SD-9: RunPod opt-in gate uses `pytest_deselected` + `items[:] = selected` true deselection. (Closed FLAG-009/all_locations-2. Settled.)
- SD-10: Block migration is a full sweep across `vibecomfy/blocks/` (subgraph, loaders, encoding, sampling, save, latent, decode, video) verified by `grep`; `_utils.node_id`/`connect` accepts `Handle`. (Settled.)
- SD-11: Doctor parser stays single-positional with `--lint` and `--allow-drift` boolean flags. `_cmd_doctor` reads via `getattr(args, '<name>', False)`. (Closed FLAG-007/correctness-2/callers-2. Settled.)
- SD-12: `run()` in-flight tracker is `asyncio.current_task()` set at top of run, cleared in finally; concurrent-run guard refuses with `RuntimeError`; `stop(wait_for_inflight=True)` awaits the entire task. (Closed callers-2/FLAG-010. Settled.)
- SD-13: `docs/authoring.md` is updated in this PR. (Settled.)
- SD-14: `vibecomfy.runtime` re-exports `EmbeddedSession`, `ServerSession`, `SessionConfig`, `RunResult` (NOT `ExternalServerRestartRequired` per SD-8). (Settled.)
- SD-15: Lockfile module lives at `vibecomfy/node_packs_lockfile.py` (sibling, no package conversion). `read_lockfile(absent_path)` returns `[]` cleanly. (Closed FLAG-006/correctness-1/all_locations-1. Settled.)
- SD-16: Doctor lockfile drift scope: lockfile absent → skip; pack dir absent → warning only; pack dir present + SHA mismatch → fail-closed unless `--allow-drift`. ACTIVE by default per brief B5. (Closed FLAG-008/scope. Settled.)
- SD-17: `_read_doctor_lockfile()` module-level helper in `vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py` is the single seam for test isolation. Existing direct `_cmd_doctor` callers in `tests/test_cli.py:285` and `tests/test_doctor_models.py:54,76` monkeypatch this helper to return `[]`. (Closed FLAG-012/iter-5 doctor-contract contradiction. Settled in iter-6.)

        Requirements:
        - Verify each success criterion explicitly.
        - Trust executor evidence by default. Dig deeper only where the git diff, `execution_audit.json`, or vague notes make the claim ambiguous.
        - Each criterion has a `priority` (`must`, `should`, or `info`). Apply these rules:
          - `must` criteria are hard gates. A `must` criterion that fails means `needs_rework`.
          - `should` criteria are quality targets. If the spirit is met but the letter is not, mark `pass` with evidence explaining the gap. Only mark `fail` if the intent was clearly missed. A `should` failure alone does NOT require `needs_rework`.
          - `info` criteria are for human reference. Mark them `waived` with a note — do not evaluate them.
          - If a criterion has `requires` capabilities that are not satisfiable by container workers (e.g., `drive_browser`, `subjective_judgment`), mark it `deferred_human` — NOT `fail` or `waived`. Deferred-human criteria do NOT count toward `needs_rework`.
          - If a criterion (any priority) cannot be verified in this context (e.g., requires manual testing or runtime observation), mark it `waived` with an explanation.
        - Set `review_verdict` to `needs_rework` only when at least one `must` criterion fails or actual implementation work is incomplete. Use `approved` when all `must` criteria pass, even if some `should` criteria are flagged.
        - The decisions listed above were settled at the gate stage. Verify that the executor implemented each settled decision correctly. Flag deviations from these decisions, but do not question the decisions themselves.
        - baseline_test_failures in finalize.json lists tests that were already failing before execution. Do not flag these as rework items unless the executor introduced new failures in those same tests.
        - Cross-reference each task's `files_changed` and `commands_run` against the git diff and any audit findings.
        - Review every `sense_check` explicitly and treat perfunctory acknowledgments as a reason to dig deeper.
        - Follow this JSON shape exactly:
        ```json
        {
          "review_verdict": "approved",
          "criteria": [
            {
              "name": "All existing tests pass",
              "priority": "must",
              "pass": "pass",
              "evidence": "Test suite ran green — 42 passed, 0 failed."
            },
            {
              "name": "File under ~300 lines",
              "priority": "should",
              "pass": "pass",
              "evidence": "File is 375 lines — above the target but reasonable given the component's responsibilities. Spirit met."
            },
            {
              "name": "Manual smoke tests pass",
              "priority": "info",
              "pass": "waived",
              "evidence": "Cannot be verified in automated review. Noted for manual QA."
            }
          ],
          "issues": [],
          "rework_items": [],
          "summary": "Approved. All must criteria pass. The should criterion on line count is close enough given the component scope.",
          "task_verdicts": [
            {
              "task_id": "T6",
              "reviewer_verdict": "Pass. Claimed handler changes and command evidence match the repo state.",
              "evidence_files": ["megaplan/handlers.py", "megaplan/evaluation.py"]
            }
          ],
          "sense_check_verdicts": [
            {
              "sense_check_id": "SC6",
              "verdict": "Confirmed. The execute blocker only fires when both evidence arrays are empty."
            }
          ]
        }
        ```
        - `rework_items` must be an array of structured rework directives. When `review_verdict` is `needs_rework`, populate one entry per issue with:
          - `task_id`: which finalize task this issue relates to
          - `issue`: what is wrong
          - `expected`: what correct behavior looks like
          - `actual`: what was observed
          - `evidence_file` (optional): file path supporting the finding
          - `flag_id`: critique/review flag ID when applicable, otherwise `null`
          - `source`: short machine-readable source tag when applicable, otherwise `null`
        - `issues` must still be populated as a flat one-line-per-item summary derived from `rework_items` (for backward compatibility). When approved, both `issues` and `rework_items` should be empty arrays.
        - When the work needs another execute pass, keep the same shape and change only `review_verdict` to `needs_rework`; make `issues`, `rework_items`, `summary`, and task verdicts specific enough for the executor to act on directly.
